# nyzhuoo.github.io[titik aman (2).html](https://github.com/user-attachments/files/33726372/titik.aman.2.html)
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="id">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover">
<title>Evakuasi Karhutla</title>
<script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com/3.4.17"></script>
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/react/18.3.1/umd/react.production.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/react-dom/18.3.1/umd/react-dom.production.min.js"></script>
<style>
  :root { --page-bg: #e5e7eb; box-sizing: border-box; }
  @media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) { :root:not([data-theme="light"]) { --page-bg: #0a0a0a; } }
  :root[data-theme="dark"] { --page-bg: #0a0a0a; }
  html, body, #root { height: 100%; margin: 0; }
  body { background: var(--page-bg); }
  @keyframes sheet-up { from { transform: translateY(100%); } to { transform: translateY(0); } }
  .sheet-up { animation: sheet-up .25s ease-out; }
  @keyframes step-in { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(8px); } to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); } }
  .step-in { animation: step-in .35s ease-out; }
  .map-grid { background-color: #f3f4f6; background-size: 32px 32px;
    background-image: linear-gradient(rgba(0,0,0,.07) 1px, transparent 1px), linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(0,0,0,.07) 1px, transparent 1px); }
  @media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) { .map-grid { background-color: #1f2937;
    background-image: linear-gradient(rgba(255,255,255,.07) 1px, transparent 1px), linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255,255,255,.07) 1px, transparent 1px); } }
  :root { --bm-land:#e4eae6; --bm-wat:#9cc3d4; --bm-r1:#e7a23c; --bm-r2:#f0c46b; --bm-r3:#ffffff; --bm-r4:#cdd8d4; --bm-smoke:90,100,105; --bm-geo:#4f625f; }
  @media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) { :root:not([data-theme="light"]) { --bm-land:#18211f; --bm-wat:#27566b; --bm-r1:#b9812a; --bm-r2:#8a7340; --bm-r3:#46544f; --bm-r4:#2b3735; --bm-smoke:200,210,210; --bm-geo:#9fb3af; } }
  :root[data-theme="dark"] { --bm-land:#18211f; --bm-wat:#27566b; --bm-r1:#b9812a; --bm-r2:#8a7340; --bm-r3:#46544f; --bm-r4:#2b3735; --bm-smoke:200,210,210; --bm-geo:#9fb3af; }
  .bm-land { fill: var(--bm-land); } .bm-wat { fill: var(--bm-wat); }
  .bm-riv { fill: none; stroke: var(--bm-wat); stroke-width: 2; vector-effect: non-scaling-stroke; }
  .bm-rd { fill: none; stroke-linecap: round; stroke-linejoin: round; vector-effect: non-scaling-stroke; transition: stroke-width .9s; }
  .bm-r1 { stroke: var(--bm-r1); stroke-width: calc(3.4px * var(--rw, 1)); } .bm-r2 { stroke: var(--bm-r2); stroke-width: calc(2.4px * var(--rw, 1)); } .bm-r3 { stroke: var(--bm-r3); stroke-width: calc(1.5px * var(--rw, 1)); } .bm-r4 { stroke: var(--bm-r4); stroke-width: calc(.8px * var(--rw, 1)); }
  .bm-smk { fill: rgb(var(--bm-smoke)); fill-opacity: .38; stroke: rgb(var(--bm-smoke)); stroke-opacity: .6; stroke-dasharray: 6 5; vector-effect: non-scaling-stroke; }
  .bm-geo { font-size: 22px; font-weight: 600; font-style: italic; fill: var(--bm-geo); }
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div id="root"></div>

<script>
/* ============================================================
   EVAKUASI KARHUTLA - TAHAP 1 (Kerangka + Bottom Navigation)
   Ditulis tanpa JSX/Babel agar langsung jalan di halaman ini.
   Setiap `h("div", {className}, ...anak)` setara dengan
   `<div className>...anak</div>` di JSX.
   ============================================================ */
const { useState } = React;
const h = React.createElement;

/* ---------- Ikon (path SVG bawaan Lucide) ---------- */
const ICONS = {
  wind: '<path d="M12.8 19.6A2 2 0 1 0 14 16H2"/><path d="M17.5 8a2.5 2.5 0 1 1 2 4H2"/><path d="M9.8 4.4A2 2 0 1 1 11 8H2"/>',
  plus: '<path d="M5 12h14"/><path d="M12 5v14"/>',
  minus: '<path d="M5 12h14"/>',
  fit: '<path d="M8 3H5a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v3"/><path d="M21 8V5a2 2 0 0 0-2-2h-3"/><path d="M3 16v3a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h3"/><path d="M16 21h3a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-3"/>',
  wifi: '<path d="M12 20h.01"/><path d="M2 8.82a15 15 0 0 1 20 0"/><path d="M5 12.859a10 10 0 0 1 14 0"/><path d="M8.5 16.429a5 5 0 0 1 7 0"/>',
  wifiOff: '<path d="M12 20h.01"/><path d="M8.5 16.429a5 5 0 0 1 7 0"/><path d="M5 12.859a10 10 0 0 1 5.17-2.69"/><path d="M19 12.859a10 10 0 0 0-2.007-1.523"/><path d="M2 8.82a15 15 0 0 1 4.177-2.643"/><path d="M22 8.82a15 15 0 0 0-11.288-3.764"/><path d="m2 2 20 20"/>',
  clock: '<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><polyline points="12 6 12 12 16 14"/>',
  ban: '<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m4.9 4.9 14.2 14.2"/>',
  tree: '<path d="m17 14 3 3.3a1 1 0 0 1-.7 1.7H4.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7-1.7L7 14h-.3a1 1 0 0 1-.7-1.7L9 9h-.2A1 1 0 0 1 8 7.3L12 3l4 4.3a1 1 0 0 1-.8 1.7H15l3 3.3a1 1 0 0 1-.7 1.7H17Z"/><path d="M12 22v-3"/>',
  cloud: '<path d="M17.5 19H9a7 7 0 1 1 6.71-9h1.79a4.5 4.5 0 1 1 0 9Z"/>',
  arrowUp: '<path d="m5 12 7-7 7 7"/><path d="M12 19V5"/>',
  turnLeft: '<polyline points="9 14 4 9 9 4"/><path d="M20 20v-7a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H4"/>',
  turnRight: '<polyline points="15 14 20 9 15 4"/><path d="M4 20v-7a4 4 0 0 1 4-4h12"/>',
  navigation: '<polygon points="3 11 22 2 13 21 11 13 3 11"/>',
  cross: '<path d="M4 9a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v2a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h4a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v4a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h2a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-4a1 1 0 0 1 1-1h4a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-2a2 2 0 0 0-2-2h-4a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V4a2 2 0 0 0-2-2h-2a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v4a1 1 0 0 1-1 1z"/>',
  x: '<path d="M18 6 6 18"/><path d="m6 6 12 12"/>',
  alert: '<path d="m21.73 18-8-14a2 2 0 0 0-3.48 0l-8 14A2 2 0 0 0 4 21h16a2 2 0 0 0 1.73-3"/><path d="M12 9v4"/><path d="M12 17h.01"/>',
  check: '<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"/>',
  siren: '<path d="M7 18v-6a5 5 0 1 1 10 0v6"/><path d="M5 21a1 1 0 0 1 1-1h12a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v1a1 1 0 0 1-1 1H6a1 1 0 0 1-1-1z"/><path d="M21 12h1"/><path d="M18.5 4.5 18 5"/><path d="M2 12h1"/><path d="M12 2v1"/><path d="m4.929 4.929.707.707"/><path d="M12 12v6"/>',
  car: '<path d="M19 17h2c.6 0 1-.4 1-1v-3c0-.9-.7-1.7-1.5-1.9C18.7 10.6 16 10 16 10s-1.3-1.4-2.2-2.3c-.5-.4-1.1-.7-1.8-.7H5c-.6 0-1.1.4-1.4.9l-1.4 2.9A3.7 3.7 0 0 0 2 12v4c0 .6.4 1 1 1h2"/><circle cx="7" cy="17" r="2"/><path d="M9 17h6"/><circle cx="17" cy="17" r="2"/>',
  pulse: '<path d="M19 14c1.49-1.46 3-3.21 3-5.5A5.5 5.5 0 0 0 16.5 3c-1.76 0-3 .5-4.5 2-1.5-1.5-2.74-2-4.5-2A5.5 5.5 0 0 0 2 8.5c0 2.3 1.5 4.05 3 5.5l7 7Z"/><path d="M3.22 12H9.5l.5-1 2 4.5 2-7 1.5 3.5h5.27"/>',
  send: '<path d="m22 2-7 20-4-9-9-4Z"/><path d="M22 2 11 13"/>',
  loader: '<path d="M21 12a9 9 0 1 1-6.219-8.56"/>',
  home: '<path d="m3 9 9-7 9 7v11a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2z"/><polyline points="9 22 9 12 15 12 15 22"/>',
  map: '<polygon points="3 6 9 3 15 6 21 3 21 18 15 21 9 18 3 21"/><line x1="9" x2="9" y1="3" y2="18"/><line x1="15" x2="15" y1="6" y2="21"/>',
  megaphone: '<path d="m3 11 18-5v12L3 14v-3z"/><path d="M11.6 16.8a3 3 0 1 1-5.8-1.6"/>',
  user: '<path d="M19 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H9a4 4 0 0 0-4 4v2"/><circle cx="12" cy="7" r="4"/>',
  flame: '<path d="M8.5 14.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 11 12c0-1.38-.5-2-1-3-1.072-2.143-.224-4.054 2-6 .5 2.5 2 4.9 4 6.5 2 1.6 3 3.5 3 5.5a7 7 0 1 1-14 0c0-1.153.433-2.294 1-3a2.5 2.5 0 0 0 2.5 2.5z"/>',
};

function Icon({ name, size = 24, strokeWidth = 2 }) {
  return h("svg", {
    xmlns: "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg",
    width: size, height: size, viewBox: "0 0 24 24",
    fill: "none", stroke: "currentColor", strokeWidth,
    strokeLinecap: "round", strokeLinejoin: "round",
    dangerouslySetInnerHTML: { __html: ICONS[name] },
  });
}

/* ---------- Komponen bersama ---------- */

// Header bagian atas setiap halaman
function PageHeader({ title, subtitle }) {
  return h("header",
    { className: "shrink-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 to-red-600 text-white px-4 pb-4 pt-[calc(env(safe-area-inset-top,0px)+1rem)] shadow" },
    h("div", { className: "flex items-center gap-2" },
      h(Icon, { name: "flame", size: 22 }),
      h("h1", { className: "text-lg font-bold leading-tight" }, title)
    ),
    subtitle && h("p", { className: "mt-1 text-sm text-orange-100" }, subtitle)
  );
}

// Teks placeholder di tengah halaman (ganti dengan fitur asli nanti)
function Placeholder({ icon, text }) {
  return h("div",
    { className: "flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 p-6 text-center text-gray-400 dark:text-neutral-500" },
    h(Icon, { name: icon, size: 48, strokeWidth: 1.5 }),
    h("p", { className: "text-base" }, text)
  );
}

/* ---------- Halaman ---------- */

/* ---------- Beranda: konfigurasi & data contoh ---------- */
const STATUS_LEVELS = {
  waspada: { label: "WASPADA", text: "Titik api terdeteksi 5km dari lokasi Anda.", box: "bg-yellow-400 text-yellow-950", sub: "text-yellow-900" },
  bahaya:  { label: "BAHAYA",  text: "Titik api terdeteksi 1km dari lokasi Anda. Segera evakuasi!", box: "bg-red-600 text-white", sub: "text-red-100" },
};
const CURRENT_STATUS = "waspada"; // ganti ke "bahaya" untuk mencoba banner merah

const EMERGENCY_TYPES = [
  { id: "api",       label: "Terjebak Api",    icon: "flame" },
  { id: "kendaraan", label: "Kendaraan Mogok", icon: "car" },
  { id: "medis",     label: "Darurat Medis",   icon: "pulse" },
];
const SEND_LABEL = { idle: "Kirim Lokasi & SOS Sekarang", locating: "Mengambil Koordinat...", sending: "Mengirim Pesan..." };

/* ---------- Beranda: komponen ---------- */

// Banner status keamanan
function StatusBanner({ level }) {
  const s = STATUS_LEVELS[level];
  return h("section", { role: "status", className: "rounded-2xl p-4 shadow flex items-start gap-3 " + s.box },
    h(Icon, { name: "alert", size: 28 }),
    h("div", null,
      h("p", { className: "font-bold text-base" }, "Status: " + s.label),
      h("p", { className: "text-sm mt-0.5 " + s.sub }, s.text)
    )
  );
}

// Indikator kesiapan data offline
function OfflineBadge() {
  return h("div", { className: "flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-green-300 bg-green-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-green-800 dark:bg-green-950 dark:border-green-800 dark:text-green-300" },
    h(Icon, { name: "check", size: 18 }),
    "Peta Offline & Jalur Evakuasi 100% Tersedia."
  );
}

// Tombol SOS raksasa
function SosButton({ onClick }) {
  return h("div", { className: "flex flex-col items-center gap-5" },
    h("div", { className: "relative" },
      h("span", { className: "absolute inset-0 rounded-full bg-red-500 opacity-40 animate-ping" }),
      h("button", {
        onClick, "aria-label": "Bantuan darurat SOS",
        className: "relative w-40 h-40 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-b from-red-500 to-red-700 text-white flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(239,68,68,0.6)] ring-4 ring-red-200 dark:ring-red-950 active:scale-95 transition-transform",
      },
        h(Icon, { name: "siren", size: 44 }),
        h("span", { className: "text-4xl font-extrabold tracking-widest" }, "SOS")
      )
    ),
    h("p", { className: "text-base font-bold tracking-wide text-red-600 dark:text-red-400" }, "BANTUAN DARURAT")
  );
}

// Bottom sheet SOS (dipasang di atas seluruh layar HP, termasuk nav)
// Logika offline-first sama seperti laporan: offline -> masuk Antrean (Outbox), online -> langsung terkirim.
function SosSheet({ online, onQueue, onClose }) {
  const [type, setType] = useState(null);
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const [step, setStep] = useState("idle"); // idle | locating | sending | sent | queued
  const timers = React.useRef([]);
  React.useEffect(() => () => timers.current.forEach(clearTimeout), []);
  const busy = step === "locating" || step === "sending";
  const done = step === "sent" || step === "queued";
  const queued = step === "queued";

  // Simulasi: 1 dtk ambil koordinat (GPS tetap jalan tanpa internet).
  // Online: 1 dtk kirim lalu terkirim. Offline: langsung masuk Antrean, dikirim saat sinyal kembali.
  const handleSend = () => {
    const t = EMERGENCY_TYPES.find((e) => e.id === type);
    setStep("locating");
    timers.current = [
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (online) setStep("sending");
        else {
          onQueue({ id: Date.now(), kind: "sos", label: "SOS: " + t.label, icon: "siren", note: note.trim(), time: nowTime() });
          setStep("queued");
        }
      }, 1000),
      online ? setTimeout(() => setStep("sent"), 2000) : null,
    ];
  };
  const handleClose = () => { if (!busy) onClose(); };

  const body = done
    ? h("div", { className: "flex flex-col items-center text-center gap-3 py-4" },
        h("div", { className: queued ? "text-yellow-500" : "text-green-600" }, h(Icon, { name: queued ? "alert" : "check", size: 64 })),
        h("h2", { className: "text-xl font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-neutral-100" }, queued ? "Tidak Ada Sinyal" : "SOS Berhasil Terkirim!"),
        h("p", { className: "text-gray-600 dark:text-neutral-400" }, queued
          ? "SOS disimpan di Antrean (Outbox) dan akan dikirim otomatis begitu sinyal kembali."
          : "Tim SAR sedang menuju ke lokasi. Tetap tenang."),
        h("button", { onClick: onClose, className: "mt-2 w-full rounded-xl bg-gray-900 py-3 font-semibold text-white dark:bg-neutral-100 dark:text-neutral-900" }, "Tutup")
      )
    : h(React.Fragment, null,
        h("div", { className: "flex items-center justify-between mb-4" },
          h("h2", { className: "text-lg font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-neutral-100" }, "Pilih Jenis Keadaan Darurat"),
          h("button", { onClick: handleClose, disabled: busy, "aria-label": "Tutup", className: "p-1 text-gray-400 disabled:opacity-40" }, h(Icon, { name: "x", size: 22 }))
        ),
        h("div", { role: "radiogroup", className: "grid grid-cols-3 gap-2" },
          EMERGENCY_TYPES.map((t) => {
            const sel = type === t.id;
            return h("button", {
              key: t.id, role: "radio", "aria-checked": sel, disabled: busy, onClick: () => setType(t.id),
              className: "flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border-2 px-1 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-center transition-colors " +
                (sel ? "border-red-600 bg-red-50 text-red-700 dark:bg-red-950 dark:text-red-300" : "border-gray-200 text-gray-600 dark:border-neutral-700 dark:text-neutral-300"),
            }, h(Icon, { name: t.icon, size: 26 }), t.label);
          })
        ),
        h("textarea", {
          value: note, onChange: (e) => setNote(e.target.value), disabled: busy, rows: 3,
          placeholder: "Ketik detail tambahan (opsional)...",
          className: "mt-4 w-full resize-none rounded-xl border border-gray-300 bg-white p-3 text-sm text-gray-900 placeholder-gray-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500 dark:border-neutral-700 dark:bg-neutral-800 dark:text-neutral-100",
        }),
        h("button", {
          onClick: handleSend, disabled: !type || busy,
          className: "mt-4 w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-red-600 py-3.5 font-bold text-white shadow active:scale-[0.98] transition disabled:opacity-50",
        },
          h("span", { className: busy ? "animate-spin" : "" }, h(Icon, { name: busy ? "loader" : "send", size: 20 })),
          SEND_LABEL[step]
        ),
        !online && h("p", { className: "mt-2 text-center text-xs font-medium text-yellow-700 dark:text-yellow-300" }, "Tidak ada sinyal: SOS akan disimpan di Antrean dan dikirim otomatis saat sinyal kembali."),
        !type && h("p", { className: "mt-2 text-center text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-neutral-400" }, "Pilih salah satu jenis keadaan darurat terlebih dahulu.")
      );

  return h("div", { className: "absolute inset-0 z-50 flex items-end bg-black/60", onClick: handleClose },
    h("div", {
      role: "dialog", "aria-modal": "true", onClick: (e) => e.stopPropagation(),
      className: "sheet-up w-full max-h-full overflow-y-auto rounded-t-3xl bg-white p-5 pb-[calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)+1.25rem)] shadow-2xl dark:bg-neutral-900",
    }, body)
  );
}

/* ---------- Beranda: data simulasi (live saat online, beku saat offline) ---------- */
const FEED_MS = 4000; // saat Online, data diperbarui tiap 4 detik (simulasi)
const makeFeed = (tick) => ({
  tick,
  hotspots: 142 + Math.round(14 * Math.sin(tick * 0.9)) + tick * 2,
  ispu: Math.min(260, 187 + Math.round(12 * Math.sin(tick * 0.7)) + tick * 2),
  windDeg: 135 + Math.round(18 * Math.sin(tick * 0.5)), // arah angin (derajat searah jarum jam dari utara); 135 = ke tenggara
  windKmh: 12 + Math.round(4 * Math.sin(tick * 0.6)),
  syncedAt: new Date(), // waktu data terakhir diunduh
});
const fmtClock = (d) => d.toLocaleTimeString("id-ID", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit" });
const COMPASS = ["Utara", "Timur Laut", "Timur", "Tenggara", "Selatan", "Barat Daya", "Barat", "Barat Laut"];

const ISPU_LEVELS = [
  { max: 50,  label: "Baik",               color: "#22c55e", advice: "Udara aman untuk beraktivitas di luar ruangan." },
  { max: 100, label: "Sedang",             color: "#eab308", advice: "Kelompok sensitif sebaiknya mengurangi aktivitas di luar ruangan." },
  { max: 200, label: "Tidak Sehat",        color: "#f97316", advice: "Hindari aktivitas di luar rumah. Balita, lansia, dan ibu hamil sebaiknya pindah ke ruang berudara bersih." },
  { max: 300, label: "Sangat Tidak Sehat", color: "#ef4444", advice: "Tetap di dalam ruangan, pakai masker N95 jika terpaksa keluar, dan siapkan rencana evakuasi." },
  { max: Infinity, label: "Berbahaya",     color: "#991b1b", advice: "Kondisi berbahaya. Ikuti arahan petugas dan segera menuju shelter udara bersih." },
];

// Peta contoh bergaya citra satelit Kalimantan Tengah (data ditulis di skala 815x693, diperkecil ke 600x510)
const MW = 600, MH = 510, MR = 600 / 815;
const HOTSPOT_POS = (() => {
  let seed = 11;
  const rnd = () => (seed = (seed * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647;
  const gauss = () => Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(rnd() + 1e-9)) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * rnd());
  const along = (pts, n, sd) => Array.from({ length: n }, () => {
    const t = rnd() * (pts.length - 1), i = Math.min(Math.floor(t), pts.length - 2), f = t - i;
    return { x: pts[i][0] + (pts[i + 1][0] - pts[i][0]) * f + gauss() * sd, y: pts[i][1] + (pts[i + 1][1] - pts[i][1]) * f + gauss() * sd };
  });
  const blob = (cx, cy, sx, sy, n) => Array.from({ length: n }, () => ({ x: cx + gauss() * sx, y: cy + gauss() * sy }));
  const all = [
    ...along([[30, 240], [70, 310], [110, 360], [190, 410], [260, 480], [340, 545], [430, 590]], 170, 30), // lengkung barat daya
    ...blob(230, 430, 70, 60, 110),                                                                       // pusat terpadat
    ...along([[430, 590], [520, 575], [570, 505], [625, 450], [690, 385], [735, 375]], 70, 14),           // lengan timur
    ...along([[520, 195], [575, 245], [615, 300], [655, 345]], 45, 16),                                   // lengan timur laut
    ...blob(490, 85, 45, 32, 55),                                                                         // utara (Gunung Mas)
    ...blob(300, 300, 130, 100, 30),                                                                      // sebaran acak
  ];
  for (let i = all.length - 1; i > 0; i--) { const j = Math.floor(rnd() * (i + 1)); [all[i], all[j]] = [all[j], all[i]]; }
  return all.filter((p) => p.x > 5 && p.x < 810 && p.y > 5 && p.y < 688)
    .map((p) => ({ x: +(p.x * MR).toFixed(1), y: +(p.y * MR).toFixed(1), big: rnd() < 0.35 }));
})();
const MAP_LINES = (() => { // garis batas/jalan tipis di atas hutan
  let s = 5; const r = () => (s = (s * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647;
  return Array.from({ length: 16 }, () => {
    let x = r() * MW, y = r() * MH, a = r() * 6.28, d = "M" + x.toFixed(0) + " " + y.toFixed(0);
    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) { a += (r() - 0.5) * 1.3; x += Math.cos(a) * 34; y += Math.sin(a) * 34; d += "L" + x.toFixed(0) + " " + y.toFixed(0); }
    return d;
  }).join("");
})();
const HEAT_RGB = { fmY: "250,204,21", fmO: "249,115,22", fmR: "190,35,30", fmD: "120,20,25" };
const HEAT_BLOBS = [ // [warna, cx, cy, rx, ry]: kuning (luas) -> oranye -> merah -> inti gelap
  ["fmY", 300, 330, 330, 260], ["fmY", 480, 430, 250, 190], ["fmY", 570, 230, 180, 150], ["fmY", 230, 110, 210, 100], ["fmY", 140, 520, 170, 120], ["fmY", 420, 50, 180, 90],
  ["fmO", 250, 400, 230, 170], ["fmO", 345, 480, 190, 110], ["fmO", 190, 300, 130, 100],
  ["fmR", 230, 340, 100, 90], ["fmR", 265, 450, 160, 100], ["fmR", 345, 510, 120, 60],
  ["fmD", 270, 440, 80, 60],
];
const ARROW_GRID = [[300, 55], [395, 25], [210, 110], [310, 150], [420, 95], [520, 60], [620, 100], [130, 180], [250, 215], [400, 260], [520, 285], [660, 235], [330, 340], [480, 330], [150, 480], [370, 420], [560, 380]]
  .map(([x, y], i) => ({ x: x * MR, y: y * MR, jitter: (i % 3) * 6 - 6 }));
const CITIES = [
  { n: ["Palangka Raya"], x: 195, y: 383 }, { n: ["Kabupaten", "Gunung Mas"], x: 475, y: 181 },
  { n: ["Sampit"], x: 613, y: 448 }, { n: ["Banjarmasin"], x: 455, y: 605 },
];
const MAP_LBL = { fontSize: 17, fontWeight: 600, fill: "#fff", paintOrder: "stroke", stroke: "rgba(0,0,0,.6)", strokeWidth: "3.5px", strokeLinejoin: "round" };
const USER_AT = { x: 27.2, y: 58 }; // posisi "Lokasi Anda" (persen) dekat Palangka Raya
const MAP_LAYERS = [
  { id: "hot",   label: "Titik Panas", dot: "bg-red-500" },
  { id: "smoke", label: "Sebaran Asap", dot: "bg-yellow-400" },
  { id: "wind",  label: "Arah Angin",  dot: "bg-sky-300" },
];

/* ---------- Beranda: komponen ---------- */

// Banner status data: Online = real-time, Offline = data tersimpan
function DataStatus({ online, feed }) {
  return h("div", { role: "status", className: "flex items-start gap-2 rounded-xl border px-3 py-2.5 text-sm " +
      (online ? "border-green-300 bg-green-50 text-green-800 dark:bg-green-950 dark:border-green-800 dark:text-green-300"
              : "border-yellow-300 bg-yellow-50 text-yellow-900 dark:bg-yellow-950 dark:border-yellow-700 dark:text-yellow-200") },
    h(Icon, { name: online ? "wifi" : "wifiOff", size: 20 }),
    h("div", null,
      h("p", { className: "font-semibold" }, online ? "Online: data real-time" : "Offline: menampilkan data tersimpan"),
      h("p", { className: "text-xs" }, online
        ? "Peta & kualitas udara diperbarui otomatis. Sinkron terakhir " + fmtClock(feed.syncedAt) + "."
        : "Data terakhir terunduh pukul " + fmtClock(feed.syncedAt) + ". Tidak real-time, bisa berbeda dari kondisi sekarang.")
    )
  );
}

// Peta sebaran titik api, asap (heatmap), dan arah angin (SVG bergaya citra satelit)
function FireMap({ feed, online, layers }) {
  const [z, setZ] = useState(1); // zoom 1x / 2x / 4x, berpusat di lokasi pengguna
  const th = (feed.windDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const k = Math.min(feed.tick, 10) * 6; // asap bergeser searah angin saat data diperbarui
  const dx = Math.sin(th) * k, dy = -Math.cos(th) * k;
  const dots = HOTSPOT_POS.slice(0, Math.min(HOTSPOT_POS.length, Math.round(feed.hotspots * 3)));
  const zoomBtn = "flex h-7 w-9 items-center justify-center text-gray-800 active:bg-gray-200";
  return h("div", {
    className: "relative w-full overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-emerald-900 bg-emerald-950 transition " + (online ? "" : "grayscale-[0.6]"),
    style: { aspectRatio: MW + " / " + MH },
  },
    h("div", { className: "absolute inset-0 transition-transform duration-500", style: { transform: "scale(" + z + ")", transformOrigin: USER_AT.x + "% " + USER_AT.y + "%" } },
      h("svg", { viewBox: "0 0 " + MW + " " + MH, className: "absolute inset-0 h-full w-full", "aria-hidden": true },
        h("defs", null,
          h("filter", { id: "fm-terr", x: 0, y: 0, width: "100%", height: "100%" },
            h("feTurbulence", { type: "fractalNoise", baseFrequency: "0.018 0.024", numOctaves: 4, seed: 3 }),
            h("feColorMatrix", { type: "matrix", values: "0.35 0 0 0 0.08 0 0.4 0 0 0.2 0 0 0.15 0 0.08 0 0 0 0 1" })),
          Object.entries(HEAT_RGB).map(([id, rgb]) => h("radialGradient", { key: id, id },
            [[0, 0.8], [0.55, 0.42], [1, 0]].map(([o, a]) => h("stop", { key: o, offset: o, stopColor: "rgb(" + rgb + ")", stopOpacity: a }))))
        ),
        h("rect", { width: MW, height: MH, filter: "url(#fm-terr)" }),
        h("path", { d: MAP_LINES, fill: "none", stroke: "#fff", strokeOpacity: 0.16, strokeWidth: 0.8 }),
        layers.smoke && h("g", { style: { transform: "translate(" + dx + "px," + dy + "px)", transition: "transform 3.5s linear" } },
          HEAT_BLOBS.map(([g, cx, cy, rx, ry], i) => h("ellipse", { key: i, cx: cx * MR, cy: cy * MR, rx: rx * MR, ry: ry * MR, fill: "url(#" + g + ")" }))),
        layers.wind && ARROW_GRID.map((a, i) =>
          h("g", { key: i, transform: "translate(" + a.x + " " + a.y + ")" },
            h("g", { style: { transform: "rotate(" + (feed.windDeg + a.jitter) + "deg)", transformBox: "fill-box", transformOrigin: "center", transition: "transform 1s" } },
              h("path", { d: "M0 26V-24M-6 -16 0 -26 6 -16", fill: "none", stroke: "#fff", strokeOpacity: 0.6, strokeWidth: 2.2, strokeLinecap: "round", strokeLinejoin: "round" })))),
        layers.hot && dots.map((p, i) => h("g", { key: i },
          h("circle", { cx: p.x, cy: p.y, r: p.big ? 4.2 : 3.2, fill: "#fb923c", stroke: "#dc2626", strokeWidth: 1.4 }),
          p.big && h("circle", { cx: p.x, cy: p.y, r: 1.5, fill: "#991b1b" }))),
        h("text", { x: 280, y: 215, textAnchor: "middle", style: { ...MAP_LBL, fontSize: 16, letterSpacing: "3px", fill: "rgba(255,255,255,.85)" } }, "KALIMANTAN TENGAH"),
        CITIES.map((c) => {
          const cx = c.x * MR, cy = c.y * MR;
          return h("g", { key: c.n[0] },
            h("circle", { cx, cy, r: 3.5, fill: "#fff", stroke: "#111", strokeWidth: 1.2 }),
            c.n.map((t, j) => h("text", { key: j, x: cx, y: cy - 8 - (c.n.length - 1 - j) * 17, textAnchor: "middle", style: MAP_LBL }, t)));
        })
      ),
      // Lokasi pengguna
      h("div", { className: "absolute -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2", style: { left: USER_AT.x + "%", top: USER_AT.y + "%" } },
        h("span", { className: "relative flex h-4 w-4" },
          h("span", { className: "absolute inline-flex h-full w-full animate-ping rounded-full bg-blue-400 opacity-70" }),
          h("span", { className: "relative inline-flex h-4 w-4 rounded-full border-2 border-white bg-blue-600" })),
        h("span", { className: "absolute left-1/2 top-full mt-1 -translate-x-1/2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full bg-black/60 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-white" }, "Lokasi Anda")
      )
    ),
    // Cap kondisi data
    h("span", { className: "absolute left-2 top-2 flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold " + (online ? "bg-green-500 text-white" : "bg-yellow-400 text-yellow-950") },
      online && h("span", { className: "h-1.5 w-1.5 animate-ping rounded-full bg-white" }),
      online ? "LIVE" : "Data tersimpan \u00b7 " + fmtClock(feed.syncedAt)),
    // Kompas
    h("div", { className: "absolute right-2 top-2 flex h-9 w-9 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-800/90 text-white shadow" },
      h("svg", { viewBox: "0 0 24 24", width: 14, height: 14 }, h("path", { d: "M12 2 17 14 12 11.5 7 14Z", fill: "#fff" })),
      h("span", { className: "-mt-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold leading-none" }, "N")),
    // Tombol zoom
    h("div", { className: "absolute right-2 top-12 overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-white/95 shadow" },
      h("button", { onClick: () => setZ(Math.min(4, z * 2)), "aria-label": "Perbesar", className: zoomBtn + " border-b border-gray-300" }, h(Icon, { name: "plus", size: 16 })),
      h("button", { onClick: () => setZ(Math.max(1, z / 2)), "aria-label": "Perkecil", className: zoomBtn }, h(Icon, { name: "minus", size: 16 }))),
    // Skala
    h("div", { className: "absolute bottom-2 left-2 rounded bg-black/55 px-1.5 pb-1 pt-0.5 text-[9px] font-semibold text-white" },
      h("div", { className: "flex justify-between" }, h("span", null, "0"), h("span", null, (100 / z) + " km")),
      h("div", { className: "h-1.5 w-[86px] border-x-2 border-b-2 border-white" }))
  );
}

// Dashboard kualitas udara
function AirQualityCard({ feed, online }) {
  const lv = ISPU_LEVELS.find((l) => feed.ispu <= l.max);
  const pos = Math.min(100, (feed.ispu / 300) * 100);
  return h("section", { className: "rounded-2xl bg-white p-4 shadow-sm dark:bg-neutral-900" },
    h("div", { className: "flex items-start justify-between gap-2" },
      h("h2", { className: "text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-neutral-100" }, "Kualitas udara di sekitarmu"),
      !online && h("span", { className: "shrink-0 rounded-full bg-yellow-400 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-yellow-950" }, "Data tersimpan")
    ),
    h("p", { className: "mt-1 text-lg font-extrabold", style: { color: lv.color } }, "ISPU " + feed.ispu + " \u00b7 " + lv.label),
    h("div", { className: "relative mt-2 h-2.5 rounded-full", style: { background: "linear-gradient(90deg,#22c55e 0%,#eab308 25%,#f97316 50%,#ef4444 75%,#7f1d1d 100%)" } },
      h("span", { className: "absolute top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full border-2 border-white bg-gray-900 shadow transition-all duration-1000", style: { left: pos + "%" } })),
    h("p", { className: "mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-neutral-400" }, "Terakhir diperbarui: " + fmtClock(feed.syncedAt) + " WIB (data simulasi)"),
    h("p", { className: "mt-2 text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-neutral-300" }, lv.advice)
  );
}

// Tiga angka ringkas: titik panas, arah asap, kecepatan angin
function StatTiles({ feed }) {
  const dir = COMPASS[Math.round(feed.windDeg / 45) % 8];
  const tiles = [
    ["flame", "Titik Panas", String(feed.hotspots), "text-red-500"],
    ["wind", "Asap menuju", dir, "text-sky-500"],
    ["wifi", "Angin", feed.windKmh + " km/jam", "text-gray-500"],
  ];
  return h("div", { className: "grid grid-cols-3 gap-2" },
    tiles.map(([icon, label, value, color]) => h("div", { key: label, className: "rounded-xl bg-white p-3 text-center shadow-sm dark:bg-neutral-900" },
      h("div", { className: "flex justify-center " + color }, h(Icon, { name: icon === "wifi" ? "wind" : icon, size: 22 })),
      h("p", { className: "mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-neutral-100" }, value),
      h("p", { className: "text-[11px] text-gray-500 dark:text-neutral-400" }, label)))
  );
}

/* ---------- Halaman Beranda ---------- */
// feed (data) dipegang App: terus diperbarui saat Online, berhenti (data tersimpan) saat Offline
function HomePage({ online, setOnline, feed }) {
  const [layers, setLayers] = useState({ hot: true, smoke: true, wind: true });
  return h(React.Fragment, null,
    h(PageHeader, { title: "Beranda", subtitle: "Sebaran karhutla & kualitas udara" }),
    h("div", { className: "flex flex-col gap-3 p-4" },
      h(DataStatus, { online, feed }),
      h(SignalToggle, { online, onToggle: () => setOnline(!online) }),
      h("section", { className: "rounded-2xl bg-white p-3 shadow-sm dark:bg-neutral-900" },
        h("h2", { className: "mb-2 text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-neutral-100" }, "Sebaran Karhutla"),
        h("div", { className: "mb-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2" },
          MAP_LAYERS.map((l) => h("button", {
            key: l.id, onClick: () => setLayers((prev) => ({ ...prev, [l.id]: !prev[l.id] })), "aria-pressed": layers[l.id],
            className: "flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors " +
              (layers[l.id] ? "border-gray-800 bg-gray-800 text-white dark:border-neutral-200 dark:bg-neutral-200 dark:text-neutral-900" : "border-gray-300 text-gray-500 dark:border-neutral-700 dark:text-neutral-400"),
          }, h("span", { className: "h-2 w-2 rounded-full " + l.dot }), l.label))
        ),
        h(FireMap, { feed, online, layers })
      ),
      h(AirQualityCard, { feed, online }),
      h(StatTiles, { feed }),
      h("p", { className: "pb-1 text-center text-[11px] text-gray-400 dark:text-neutral-500" }, "Prototipe ilustrasi: semua lokasi, angka, dan data pada halaman ini adalah data contoh.")
    )
  );
}

/* ---------- Peta: konfigurasi & data ---------- */
// Peta dasar (jalan, sungai, air) dari OpenStreetMap, Palangka Raya. (c) kontributor OpenStreetMap, lisensi ODbL.
// Peta dasar memakai satuan ~20 m (0..W, 0..H). Penanda & jalur memakai persen (0-100) dari kotak peta.

  const B = BASEMAP, p = (cls, d) => '<path class="' + cls + '" d="' + d + '"/>';

    [4, 3, 2, 1].map((k) => p("bm-rd bm-r" + k, B.roads[k])).join("") +
    B.smoke.map((s) => '<polygon class="bm-smk" points="' + s + '"/>').join("") +
    '<text class="bm-geo" x="' + B.label[0] + '" y="' + B.label[1] + '" text-anchor="middle">Sungai Kahayan</text>';
})();

Bukit Hindu","type":"Rumah Oksigen (Contoh)","pos":{"x":52.35,"y":51.39},"routes":[{"id":"asap","label":"Hindari Asap","km":8.3,"min":25,"note":"Memutar menjauhi area asap, hanya ±5% rute yang melewati asap.","points":"61.04,69.89 63.74,69.89 64.04,67.87 68.83,67.96 69.03,64.34 67.53,62.07 67.03,60.39 66.03,59.71 66.23,59.29 65.03,58.12 64.54,58.03 64.64,56.01 58.74,55.51 55.94,52.14 52.55,51.39 52.35,51.39"},{"id":"cepat","label":"Tercepat","km":5.9,"min":18,"note":"Jalur paling singkat, tetapi ±59% rute melewati asap.","points":"61.04,69.89 58.24,70.4 58.44,55.34 55.94,52.14 52.55,51.39 52.35,51.39"}]},{"id":"iahn","name":"Aula IAHN Tampung Penyang","short":"Aula IAHN","type":"Shelter Udara Bersih (Contoh)","pos":{"x":43.16,"y":64.84},"routes":[{"id":"cepat","label":"Tercepat","km":5.5,"min":16,"note":"Berada di area asap dan belum diverifikasi, tidak disarankan sebagai tujuan.","points":"61.04,69.89 60.44,71.66 45.65,71.49 41.96,64.84 42.96,64.42 43.16,64.84"}]}];
const STEP_MS = 4000; // jeda antar instruksi navigasi (ms)
const ZOOM_DEFAULT = 1, ZOOM_MIN = 0.8, ZOOM_MAX = 8; // batas zoom peta
const NAV_ZOOM = 4, NAV_FOCUS = 0.42; // zoom saat navigasi & posisi pengguna di layar (0.42 = 42% dari atas)
const CAM = { quick: "0.5s ease-out", intro: "1.8s cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1)", follow: "2s linear" }; // kecepatan animasi kamera

/* ---------- Peta: logika jalur ---------- */
const parsePoints = (s) => s.split(" ").map((p) => { const [x, y] = p.split(",").map(Number); return { x, y }; });

// Membuat instruksi belokan otomatis dari bentuk jalur (lurus / belok kiri / belok kanan / tiba)
function buildSteps(points, destName, totalKm) {
  const pts = parsePoints(points);
  const segs = pts.slice(1).map((b, i) => ({ a: pts[i], b, len: Math.hypot(b.x - pts[i].x, b.y - pts[i].y) }));
  const total = segs.reduce((sum, g) => sum + g.len, 0);
  const lerp = (a, b, t) => ({ x: a.x + (b.x - a.x) * t, y: a.y + (b.y - a.y) * t });
  const dirOf = (g) => Math.round(Math.atan2((g.b.x - g.a.x) * BASEMAP.W, -(g.b.y - g.a.y) * BASEMAP.H) * 180 / Math.PI); // derajat dari utara
  const fmtDist = (km) => (km < 1 ? Math.max(50, Math.round(km * 20) * 50) + " meter" : km.toFixed(1).replace(".", ",") + " km");
  const steps = [];
  let done = 0, runLen = 0, runFirst = null;
  segs.forEach((g, i) => {
    if (!runFirst) runFirst = g;
    runLen += g.len;
    const next = segs[i + 1];
    let right = false;
    if (next) {
      const cross = (g.b.x - g.a.x) * (next.b.y - next.a.y) - (g.b.y - g.a.y) * (next.b.x - next.a.x);
      const dot = (g.b.x - g.a.x) * (next.b.x - next.a.x) + (g.b.y - g.a.y) * (next.b.y - next.a.y);
      if (Math.abs(Math.atan2(cross, dot)) * 180 / Math.PI < 40) return; // belokan kecil dianggap tetap lurus
      right = cross > 0; // koordinat layar: cross > 0 = belok kanan
    }
    steps.push({ icon: "arrowUp", text: "Lurus " + fmtDist((totalKm * runLen) / total), remaining: ((totalKm * (total - done)) / total).toFixed(1) + " km", pos: lerp(runFirst.a, runFirst.b, done === 0 ? 0 : 0.1), dir: dirOf(runFirst) });
    done += runLen; runLen = 0; runFirst = null;
    if (!next) return;
    steps.push({ icon: right ? "turnRight" : "turnLeft", text: "Belok " + (right ? "Kanan" : "Kiri") + " dalam 50 meter", remaining: ((totalKm * (total - done)) / total).toFixed(1) + " km", pos: lerp(g.a, g.b, 0.85), dir: dirOf(g) });
  });
  steps.push({ icon: "check", text: "Anda telah tiba di " + destName, remaining: "0 km", pos: pts[pts.length - 1], dir: dirOf(segs[segs.length - 1]) });
  return steps;
}

/* ---------- Peta: komponen ---------- */

// Penanda di atas peta (dipusatkan pada titik x,y)
function MapMarker({ x, y, label, className = "", children }) {
  return h("div", { className: "absolute -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 " + className, style: { left: x + "%", top: y + "%" } },
    h("div", { className: "relative", style: { transform: "scale(var(--inv, 1))", transition: "transform var(--cam, 0s)" } },
      children,
      label && h("span", { className: "absolute top-full left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 mt-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full bg-white/90 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-gray-700 shadow dark:bg-neutral-900/90 dark:text-neutral-200" }, label))
  );
}

function MapLegend() {
  const rows = [["bg-blue-600", "Lokasi Anda"], ["bg-green-600", "Posko & Faskes"], ["bg-red-600", "Titik Api"], ["bg-gray-500", "Perkiraan Asap"]];
  return h("div", { className: "absolute left-3 top-3 z-10 rounded-xl bg-white/90 px-3 py-2 shadow dark:bg-neutral-900/90" },
    rows.map(([color, text]) => h("div", { key: text, className: "flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-neutral-200" },
      h("span", { className: "h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full " + color }), text))
  );
}

// Panel melayang: pilih jalur (kondisi awal) & mode navigasi aktif
function NavPanel({ navigating, steps, step, facility, route, onRoute, onStart, onStop }) {
  const s = steps[Math.min(step, steps.length - 1)];
  const arrived = step >= steps.length - 1;
  return h("div", { "data-nodrag": true, className: "absolute inset-x-3 bottom-3 z-20 max-h-[75%] overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl bg-white p-3 shadow-2xl dark:bg-neutral-900" },
    navigating
      ? h(React.Fragment, null,
          h("div", { key: step, className: "step-in flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl p-3 text-white " + (arrived ? "bg-green-600" : "bg-emerald-700") },
            h(Icon, { name: s.icon, size: 36, strokeWidth: 2.5 }),
            h("p", { className: "text-lg font-extrabold leading-tight" }, s.text)
          ),
          h("div", { className: "mt-2 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-neutral-400" },
            h("span", null, "Menuju " + facility.name + " (" + route.label + ")"), h("span", null, "Sisa " + s.remaining)),
          h("div", { className: "mt-1 h-1.5 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-gray-200 dark:bg-neutral-700" },
            h("div", { className: "h-full rounded-full bg-green-500 transition-all duration-700", style: { width: (step / (steps.length - 1)) * 100 + "%" } })),
          h("div", { className: "mt-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-green-600 dark:text-green-400" },
            h("span", { className: "relative flex h-2.5 w-2.5" },
              h("span", { className: "absolute inline-flex h-full w-full animate-ping rounded-full bg-green-500 opacity-70" }),
              h("span", { className: "relative inline-flex h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-green-600" })
            ),
            "Sinyal GPS Stabil - Mode Luring Aktif"
          ),
          h("button", { onClick: onStop, className: "mt-2 flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-white active:scale-[0.98] transition " + (arrived ? "bg-green-600" : "bg-red-600") },
            h(Icon, { name: arrived ? "check" : "x", size: 18 }), arrived ? "Selesai" : "Akhiri Navigasi")
        )
      : h(React.Fragment, null,
          h("div", { className: "flex items-center gap-3" },
            h("div", { className: "flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-green-100 text-green-700 dark:bg-green-950 dark:text-green-400" }, h(Icon, { name: "cross", size: 18 })),
            h("div", null,
              h("p", { className: "text-[11px] font-medium text-gray-500 dark:text-neutral-400" }, "Tujuan: " + facility.type + " · " + route.km.toFixed(1) + " km"),
              h("p", { className: "text-sm font-bold leading-tight text-gray-900 dark:text-neutral-100" }, facility.name)
            )
          ),
          h("div", { role: "radiogroup", "aria-label": "Pilih jalur", className: "mt-2 grid gap-1.5", style: { gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(" + facility.routes.length + ", minmax(0, 1fr))" } },
            facility.routes.map((r) => {
              const sel = r.id === route.id;
              return h("button", {
                key: r.id, role: "radio", "aria-checked": sel, onClick: () => onRoute(r.id),
                className: "rounded-xl border-2 px-1 py-1.5 text-center transition-colors " + (sel ? "border-blue-600 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 dark:bg-blue-950 dark:text-blue-300" : "border-gray-200 text-gray-600 dark:border-neutral-700 dark:text-neutral-300"),
              }, h("span", { className: "block text-xs font-bold" }, r.label), h("span", { className: "block text-[11px]" }, r.min + " mnt \u00b7 " + r.km.toFixed(1) + " km"));
            })
          ),
          h("p", { className: "mt-1.5 line-clamp-2 text-[11px] leading-snug text-gray-500 dark:text-neutral-400" }, route.note),
          h("button", { onClick: onStart, className: "mt-2 flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow active:scale-[0.98] transition" },
            h(Icon, { name: "navigation", size: 18 }), "Mulai Navigasi (Offline GPS)")
        )
  );
}

/* ---------- Halaman Peta ---------- */
function MapPage() {
  const [facilityId, setFacilityId] = useState(FACILITIES[0].id);
  const [routeId, setRouteId] = useState(FACILITIES[0].routes[0].id);
  const [navigating, setNavigating] = useState(false);
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);

  // Zoom & geser peta (tombol +/- dan seret dengan jari/mouse)
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(ZOOM_DEFAULT);
  const [pan, setPan] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [cam, setCam] = useState(CAM.quick); // kecepatan animasi kamera
  const drag = React.useRef(null), mapRef = React.useRef(null), hdg = React.useRef(0);
  const changeZoom = (d) => { setCam(navigating ? CAM.follow : CAM.quick); setZoom((z) => Math.min(ZOOM_MAX, Math.max(ZOOM_MIN, +(z * (d > 0 ? 1.25 : 0.8)).toFixed(2)))); };
  const resetView = () => { setCam(CAM.quick); if (navigating) return setZoom(NAV_ZOOM); setZoom(ZOOM_DEFAULT); setPan({ x: 0, y: 0 }); };
  const onMapDown = (e) => {
    if (navigating || e.target.closest("button, [data-nodrag]")) return; // saat navigasi kamera otomatis mengikuti pengguna
    // tombol & panel tidak memicu geser
    drag.current = { sx: e.clientX, sy: e.clientY, ox: pan.x, oy: pan.y, moved: false };
  };
  const onMapMove = (e) => {
    const d = drag.current;
    if (!d) return;
    const dx = e.clientX - d.sx, dy = e.clientY - d.sy;
    if (!d.moved) {
      if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 6) return;
      d.moved = true;
      e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    }
    const lim = 300 * Math.max(1, zoom), clamp = (v) => Math.max(-lim, Math.min(lim, v));
    setPan({ x: clamp(d.ox + dx), y: clamp(d.oy + dy) });
  };
  const onMapUp = () => { drag.current = null; };

  const facility = FACILITIES.find((f) => f.id === facilityId);
  const route = facility.routes.find((r) => r.id === routeId) || facility.routes[0];
  const steps = buildSteps(route.points, facility.name, route.km);
  const pos = steps[Math.min(step, steps.length - 1)].pos; // marker "Anda" mengikuti langkah navigasi

  // Arah hadap: diputar lewat jalur terpendek agar kerucut tidak berputar 360 derajat
  hdg.current += ((steps[Math.min(step, steps.length - 1)].dir - hdg.current + 540) % 360) - 180;

  // Kamera navigasi: posisi pengguna selalu dijaga di titik fokus layar
  React.useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const el = mapRef.current;
    if (!navigating || !el) return;
    const W = el.clientWidth, H = el.clientHeight, Bw = Math.max(W, H * BASEMAP.W / BASEMAP.H);
    const X = W / 2 + (pos.x / 100 - 0.5) * Bw, Y = (pos.y / 100) * H;
    setPan({ x: -zoom * (X - W / 2), y: H * NAV_FOCUS - zoom * Y });
  }, [navigating, pos.x, pos.y, zoom]);

  const pickFacility = (f) => { setFacilityId(f.id); setRouteId(f.routes[0].id); setStep(0); };

  // Saat navigasi aktif, maju ke instruksi berikutnya tiap STEP_MS (berhenti di langkah terakhir)
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!navigating) return;
    const id = setInterval(() => { setCam(CAM.follow); setStep((s) => Math.min(s + 1, steps.length - 1)); }, STEP_MS);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, [navigating, steps.length]);
  const startNav = () => { setStep(0); setCam(CAM.intro); setZoom(NAV_ZOOM); setNavigating(true); }; // zoom masuk ke posisi pengguna
  const stopNav = () => { setNavigating(false); setStep(0); setCam(CAM.intro); setZoom(ZOOM_DEFAULT); setPan({ x: 0, y: 0 }); }; // zoom keluar

  return h(React.Fragment, null,
    h(PageHeader, { title: "Peta", subtitle: "Jalur evakuasi & titik aman" }),
    h("div", { className: "shrink-0 px-3 pt-3" }, h(OfflineBadge)),
    // Pilihan tujuan (geser ke samping)
    h("div", { role: "radiogroup", "aria-label": "Pilih tujuan", className: "flex shrink-0 gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-3 py-3" },
      FACILITIES.map((f) => {
        const sel = f.id === facilityId;
        return h("button", {
          key: f.id, role: "radio", "aria-checked": sel, disabled: navigating, onClick: () => pickFacility(f),
          className: "shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors disabled:opacity-60 " +
            (sel ? "border-green-600 bg-green-600 text-white" : "border-gray-300 bg-white text-gray-700 dark:border-neutral-700 dark:bg-neutral-900 dark:text-neutral-200"),
        }, f.name);
      })
    ),
    h("div", { ref: mapRef, className: "map-grid relative flex-1 min-h-[420px] overflow-hidden touch-none select-none cursor-grab", onPointerDown: onMapDown, onPointerMove: onMapMove, onPointerUp: onMapUp, onPointerCancel: onMapUp },
      // Lapisan peta (ikut di-zoom & digeser)
      h("div", { className: "absolute inset-0", style: { transformOrigin: "50% 0%", transform: "translate(" + pan.x + "px, " + pan.y + "px) scale(" + zoom + ")", transition: drag.current && drag.current.moved ? "none" : "transform " + cam, "--cam": cam, "--inv": 1 / zoom, "--rw": 1 + (zoom - 1) * 0.3 } },
      // Kotak peta (rasio tetap agar jalan tidak mulur): peta dasar OSM, sebaran asap, jalur, titik api
      h("div", { className: "absolute top-0 left-1/2 h-full", style: { aspectRatio: BASEMAP.W + " / " + BASEMAP.H, minWidth: "100%", transform: "translateX(-50%)" } },
      h("svg", { className: "absolute inset-0 h-full w-full", viewBox: "0 0 " + BASEMAP.W + " " + BASEMAP.H, preserveAspectRatio: "none", "aria-hidden": "true", dangerouslySetInnerHTML: { __html: BASEMAP_SVG } }),
      h("svg", { className: "absolute inset-0 h-full w-full", viewBox: "0 0 100 100", preserveAspectRatio: "none", "aria-hidden": "true" },
        !navigating && facility.routes.filter((r) => r.id !== route.id).map((r) =>
          h("polyline", { key: r.id, points: r.points, fill: "none", stroke: "#93c5fd", strokeWidth: 3, strokeDasharray: "3 6", strokeLinecap: "round", strokeLinejoin: "round", vectorEffect: "non-scaling-stroke" })),
        h("polyline", { points: route.points, fill: "none", stroke: "#ffffff", strokeWidth: navigating ? 16 : 8, strokeLinecap: "round", strokeLinejoin: "round", vectorEffect: "non-scaling-stroke" }),
        h("polyline", {
          points: route.points, fill: "none", stroke: "#2563eb", strokeWidth: navigating ? 10 : 4,
          strokeDasharray: navigating ? "none" : "8 6", strokeLinecap: "round", strokeLinejoin: "round", vectorEffect: "non-scaling-stroke",
        })
      ),
      // Titik api (karhutla) dan label sebaran asap
      HOTSPOTS.map((p, i) => h(MapMarker, { key: "hs" + i, x: p.x, y: p.y, label: i === 0 ? "Titik Api" : null },
        h("span", { className: "absolute left-1/2 top-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-9 w-9 animate-ping rounded-full bg-red-500/40" }),
        h("div", { className: "relative flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-red-600 text-white shadow ring-2 ring-white" }, h(Icon, { name: "flame", size: 12 }))
      )),
      h(MapMarker, { x: SMOKE_LABEL.x, y: SMOKE_LABEL.y, label: "Perkiraan Asap" },
        h("div", { className: "flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gray-500 text-white shadow ring-2 ring-white" }, h(Icon, { name: "cloud", size: 16 }))
      ),
      // Posko & fasilitas kesehatan (ketuk untuk memilih tujuan)
      FACILITIES.map((f) => {
        const sel = f.id === facilityId;
        return h(MapMarker, { key: f.id, x: f.pos.x, y: f.pos.y, label: navigating && !sel ? null : f.short, className: sel ? "z-[5]" : "" },
          h("button", {
            onClick: () => pickFacility(f), disabled: navigating, "aria-label": "Pilih " + f.name,
            className: "relative flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-green-600 text-white shadow transition-all before:absolute before:-inset-2 before:content-[''] " +
              (sel ? "h-6 w-6 ring-2 ring-green-300" : "h-4 w-4 ring-1 ring-white " + (navigating ? "opacity-30" : "opacity-90")),
          }, h(Icon, { name: "cross", size: sel ? 14 : 10 }))
        );
      }),
      // Lokasi pengguna (titik biru berdenyut)
      h(MapMarker, { x: pos.x, y: pos.y, label: "Anda", className: "z-[6] transition-[left,top] duration-[2000ms] ease-linear" },
        navigating && h("span", { className: "absolute left-1/2 top-1/2 h-0 w-0", style: { transform: "rotate(" + hdg.current + "deg)", transition: "transform 1s" } },
          h("span", { className: "absolute -translate-x-1/2 bg-gradient-to-t from-blue-500/60 to-transparent", style: { width: 64, height: 64, bottom: 0, clipPath: "polygon(50% 100%, 0 0, 100% 0)" } })),
        h("span", { className: "relative flex h-5 w-5" },
          h("span", { className: "absolute inline-flex h-full w-full animate-ping rounded-full bg-blue-500 opacity-60" }),
          h("span", { className: "relative inline-flex h-5 w-5 rounded-full border-2 border-white bg-blue-600 shadow" })
        )
      ),
      ),
      ),
      h(MapLegend),
      h("div", { "data-nodrag": true, className: "absolute right-3 top-3 z-10 flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-white/90 text-gray-700 shadow dark:bg-neutral-900/90 dark:text-neutral-200" },
        [["plus", "Perbesar peta", () => changeZoom(0.2)], ["minus", "Perkecil peta", () => changeZoom(-0.2)], ["fit", "Atur ulang tampilan peta", resetView]].map(([icon, label, fn]) =>
          h("button", { key: icon, onClick: fn, "aria-label": label, className: "flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center active:bg-gray-200 dark:active:bg-neutral-700" }, h(Icon, { name: icon, size: 20 })))
      ),
      h(NavPanel, { navigating, steps, step, facility, route, onRoute: (id) => { setRouteId(id); setStep(0); }, onStart: startNav, onStop: stopNav })
    )
  );
}

/* ---------- Lapor: konfigurasi ---------- */
const REPORT_TYPES = [
  { id: "jalan", label: "Jalan Terputus", icon: "ban" },
  { id: "pohon", label: "Pohon Tumbang",  icon: "tree" },
  { id: "asap",  label: "Asap Tebal",     icon: "cloud" },
  { id: "api",   label: "Api Mendekat",   icon: "flame" },
];
const NOTICE_STYLES = {
  success: "border-green-300 bg-green-50 text-green-800 dark:bg-green-950 dark:border-green-800 dark:text-green-300",
  warning: "border-yellow-300 bg-yellow-50 text-yellow-900 dark:bg-yellow-950 dark:border-yellow-700 dark:text-yellow-200",
};
const nowTime = () => new Date().toLocaleTimeString("id-ID", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });

/* ---------- Lapor: komponen ---------- */

// Sakelar simulasi sinyal internet
function SignalToggle({ online, onToggle }) {
  return h("section", { className: "flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl bg-white p-4 shadow-sm dark:bg-neutral-900" },
    h("div", { className: "flex items-center gap-3" },
      h("div", { className: "flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full " + (online ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700 dark:bg-green-950 dark:text-green-400" : "bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 dark:bg-neutral-800 dark:text-neutral-400") },
        h(Icon, { name: online ? "wifi" : "wifiOff", size: 22 })),
      h("div", null,
        h("p", { className: "text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900 dark:text-neutral-100" }, "Simulasi Sinyal Internet"),
        h("p", { className: "text-xs font-medium " + (online ? "text-green-600 dark:text-green-400" : "text-gray-500 dark:text-neutral-400") }, online ? "Online" : "Offline")
      )
    ),
    h("button", {
      role: "switch", "aria-checked": online, "aria-label": "Simulasi sinyal internet", onClick: onToggle,
      className: "relative h-8 w-14 shrink-0 rounded-full transition-colors " + (online ? "bg-green-500" : "bg-gray-400 dark:bg-neutral-600"),
    }, h("span", { className: "absolute left-1 top-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-white shadow transition-transform " + (online ? "translate-x-6" : "translate-x-0") }))
  );
}

// Notifikasi hasil pengiriman (hijau = sukses, kuning = antrean)
function Notice({ notice }) {
  return h("div", { role: "status", className: "step-in flex items-start gap-2 rounded-xl border px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium " + NOTICE_STYLES[notice.kind] },
    h(Icon, { name: notice.kind === "success" ? "check" : "alert", size: 20 }),
    h("span", null, notice.text)
  );
}

// Daftar laporan tertunda (outbox)
function OutboxList({ outbox }) {
  return h("section", { className: "rounded-2xl bg-white p-4 shadow-sm dark:bg-neutral-900" },
    h("div", { className: "mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2" },
      h("h2", { className: "text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-neutral-100" }, "Laporan Tertunda (Menunggu Sinyal)"),
      h("span", { className: "rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold " + (outbox.length ? "bg-yellow-400 text-yellow-950" : "bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 dark:bg-neutral-800") }, outbox.length)
    ),
    outbox.length === 0
      ? h("p", { className: "py-3 text-center text-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-neutral-500" }, "Tidak ada laporan tertunda.")
      : h("ul", { className: "flex flex-col gap-2" },
          outbox.map((r) => h("li", { key: r.id, className: "step-in flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border p-3 " + (r.kind === "sos" ? "border-red-300 bg-red-50 dark:border-red-800 dark:bg-red-950" : "border-yellow-200 bg-yellow-50 dark:border-yellow-800 dark:bg-yellow-950") },
            h("div", { className: r.kind === "sos" ? "text-red-600 dark:text-red-400" : "text-yellow-700 dark:text-yellow-300" }, h(Icon, { name: r.icon, size: 24 })),
            h("div", { className: "flex-1" },
              h("p", { className: "text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900 dark:text-neutral-100" }, r.label),
              h("p", { className: "text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-neutral-400" }, "Disimpan " + r.time + (r.note ? " \u00b7 " + r.note : ""))
            ),
            h("span", { className: "flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300" }, h(Icon, { name: "clock", size: 14 }), "Menunggu")
          ))
        )
  );
}

/* ---------- Halaman Lapor ---------- */
// online & outbox dipegang App agar tidak hilang saat pindah tab
function ReportPage({ online, setOnline, outbox, setOutbox }) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(null);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState(null);
  const prevOnline = React.useRef(online);
  const [sosOpen, setSosOpen] = useState(false);

  // Antrean: SOS selalu di urutan paling atas (urutan lainnya tetap)
  const queueItem = (item) => setOutbox((prev) => [...prev, item].sort((a, b) => (b.kind === "sos") - (a.kind === "sos")));
  const queueSos = (item) => {
    queueItem(item);
    setNotice({ kind: "warning", text: "Tidak ada sinyal. SOS disimpan di Antrean (Outbox).", id: Date.now() });
  };

  // Sinkronisasi: Offline -> Online dan ada antrean => kosongkan outbox
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!prevOnline.current && online && outbox.length > 0) {
      setOutbox([]);
      setNotice({ kind: "success", text: outbox.some((r) => r.kind === "sos") ? "Sinkronisasi berhasil! SOS dan laporan tertunda telah terkirim." : "Sinkronisasi berhasil! Laporan tertunda telah terkirim.", id: Date.now() });
    }
    prevOnline.current = online;
  }, [online]);

  // Notifikasi hilang otomatis setelah 5 detik
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!notice) return;
    const id = setTimeout(() => setNotice(null), 5000);
    return () => clearTimeout(id);
  }, [notice]);

  const handleSubmit = () => {
    const type = REPORT_TYPES.find((t) => t.id === selected);
    if (!type) return;
    if (online) {
      setNotice({ kind: "success", text: "Laporan langsung terkirim ke server pemerintah!", id: Date.now() });
    } else {
      queueItem({ id: Date.now(), kind: "report", label: type.label, icon: type.icon, time: nowTime() });
      setNotice({ kind: "warning", text: "Tidak ada sinyal. Laporan disimpan di Antrean (Outbox).", id: Date.now() });
    }
    setSelected(null);
  };

  return h(React.Fragment, null,
    h(PageHeader, { title: "Lapor", subtitle: "Laporkan titik api atau kondisi darurat" }),
    h("div", { className: "flex flex-col gap-4 p-4" },
      h(SignalToggle, { online, onToggle: () => setOnline(!online) }),
      notice && h(Notice, { key: notice.id, notice }),
      h("section", { className: "flex justify-center py-2" }, h(SosButton, { onClick: () => setSosOpen(true) })),
      h("section", { className: "rounded-2xl bg-white p-4 shadow-sm dark:bg-neutral-900" },
        h("h2", { className: "mb-3 text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-neutral-100" }, "Jenis Kendala"),
        h("div", { role: "radiogroup", className: "grid grid-cols-2 gap-2" },
          REPORT_TYPES.map((t) => {
            const sel = selected === t.id;
            return h("button", {
              key: t.id, role: "radio", "aria-checked": sel, onClick: () => setSelected(t.id),
              className: "flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border-2 px-3 py-3 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors " +
                (sel ? "border-orange-500 bg-orange-50 text-orange-700 dark:bg-orange-950 dark:text-orange-300" : "border-gray-200 text-gray-600 dark:border-neutral-700 dark:text-neutral-300"),
            }, h(Icon, { name: t.icon, size: 22 }), t.label);
          })
        ),
        h("button", {
          onClick: handleSubmit, disabled: !selected,
          className: "mt-4 flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-orange-600 py-3.5 font-bold text-white shadow transition active:scale-[0.98] disabled:opacity-50",
        }, h(Icon, { name: "send", size: 20 }), "Kirim Laporan"),
        !selected && h("p", { className: "mt-2 text-center text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-neutral-400" }, "Pilih salah satu jenis kendala terlebih dahulu.")
      ),
      h(OutboxList, { outbox })
    ),
    sosOpen && h(SosSheet, { online, onQueue: queueSos, onClose: () => setSosOpen(false) })
  );
}

function ProfilePage() {
  return h(React.Fragment, null,
    h(PageHeader, { title: "Profil", subtitle: "Data diri & pengaturan" }),
    h(Placeholder, { icon: "user", text: "Konten Profil akan ada di sini" })
  );
}

/* ---------- Konfigurasi navigasi ---------- */
// Tambah/ubah menu cukup lewat array ini.
const NAV_ITEMS = [
  { id: "home",    label: "Beranda", icon: "home",      Page: HomePage },
  { id: "map",     label: "Peta",    icon: "map",       Page: MapPage },
  { id: "report",  label: "Lapor",   icon: "megaphone", Page: ReportPage },
  { id: "profile", label: "Profil",  icon: "user",      Page: ProfilePage },
];

/* ---------- Bottom Navigation Bar ---------- */
function BottomNav({ active, onChange }) {
  return h("nav",
    { className: "shrink-0 border-t border-gray-200 bg-white dark:bg-neutral-900 dark:border-neutral-800 pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)]" },
    h("ul", { className: "flex" },
      NAV_ITEMS.map(({ id, label, icon }) => {
        const isActive = active === id;
        return h("li", { key: id, className: "flex-1" },
          h("button", {
            onClick: () => onChange(id),
            "aria-current": isActive ? "page" : undefined,
            className: "w-full flex flex-col items-center gap-1 py-2.5 text-xs transition-colors " +
              (isActive ? "text-orange-600 font-semibold" : "text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 dark:text-neutral-500"),
          },
            h(Icon, { name: icon, size: 24, strokeWidth: isActive ? 2.5 : 2 }),
            h("span", null, label),
            h("span", { className: "h-0.5 w-6 rounded-full " + (isActive ? "bg-orange-600" : "bg-transparent") })
          )
        );
      })
    )
  );
}

/* ---------- App (kontainer mobile-first + routing useState) ---------- */
function App() {
  const [activePage, setActivePage] = useState("home");
  // Status sinyal & antrean laporan dipegang di sini agar bertahan saat pindah tab
  const [online, setOnline] = useState(true);
  const [outbox, setOutbox] = useState([]);
  // Data karhutla & udara: diperbarui otomatis hanya saat Online; saat Offline tetap data terakhir yang terunduh
  const [feed, setFeed] = useState(() => makeFeed(0));
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!online) return;
    setFeed((f) => makeFeed(f.tick + 1));
    const id = setInterval(() => setFeed((f) => makeFeed(f.tick + 1)), FEED_MS);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, [online]);
  const { Page } = NAV_ITEMS.find((item) => item.id === activePage);

  return h("div",
    { className: "max-w-md mx-auto h-full relative flex flex-col overflow-hidden border border-gray-300 dark:border-neutral-800 shadow-lg bg-gray-50 dark:bg-neutral-950" },
    h("main", { className: "flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-y-auto" }, h(Page, { online, setOnline, outbox, setOutbox, feed })),
    h(BottomNav, { active: activePage, onChange: setActivePage })
  );
}

ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById("root")).render(h(App));
</script>
</body>
</html>
